{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_5","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport pandas as pd, numpy as np\nfrom scipy.stats import pearsonr\nfrom pathlib import Path\np=Path('/app/dataset')\ns=pd.read_csv(p/'stations.csv').set_index('station_id')\nr=pd.read_csv(p/'readings.csv',parse_dates=['timestamp'])\n# stations x timestamps, preserve gaps then interpolate up to 2 interior points, edge nearest\npoll=r.groupby('station_id').pollutant.first()\ntimes=sorted(r.timestamp.unique())\nwide=r.pivot(index='station_id',columns='timestamp',values='current_reading').reindex(columns=times)\nfill=wide.T.interpolate(limit=2,limit_area='inside').T\n# coordinate distance approximation km\nlat=s.latitude.to_numpy(); lon=s.longitude.to_numpy(); ids=s.index.to_numpy()\nD=np.sqrt(((lat[:,None]-lat[None,:])*111)**2+((lon[:,None]-lon[None,:])*70)**2)\ndf=[]\nfor sid in wide.index:\n    i=np.where(ids==sid)[0][0]\n    eligible=np.where((poll.reindex(ids).values==poll[sid]) & (ids\"'!=sid))[0]\n    nn=eligible[np.argsort(D[i,eligible])[:8]]\n    # inverse dist weighted peer mean; nan-aware\n    vals=fill.reindex(ids[nn]).to_numpy(float)\n    weights=1/np.maximum(D[i,nn],.1)\n    valid=np.isfinite(vals)\n    peer=np.nansum(vals*weights[:,None],axis=0)/np.nansum(valid*weights[:,None],axis=0)\n    x=fill.loc[sid].to_numpy(float)\n    ok=np.isfinite(x)&np.isfinite(peer)\n    def cor(a,b):\n      m=np.isfinite(a)&np.isfinite(b)\n      return np.corrcoef(a[m],b[m])[0,1] if m.sum()>=6 else np.nan\n    c0=cor(x,peer)\n    lagcors={lag: cor(x[max(lag,0):len(x)+min(lag,0)],peer[max(-lag,0):len(peer)-max(lag,0)]) for lag in range(-4,5)}\n    # Here positive lag tests x[t] vs peer[t-lag], x delayed peer\n    bestlag=max((k for k in lagcors if k!=0),key=lambda k:lagcors[k])\n    slope=np.polyfit(peer[ok],x[ok],1)[0]\n    resid=x-(np.nanmean(x)+(peer-np.nanmean(peer))*slope)\n    dx=np.diff(x); dp=np.diff(peer); mok=np.isfinite(dx)&np.isfinite(dp)\n    diff_slope=np.polyfit(dp[mok],dx[mok],1)[0]\n    df.append(dict(sid=sid,poll=poll[sid],district=s.loc[sid,'\"'district'],n=wide.loc[sid].notna().sum(),c0=c0,bestlag=bestlag,bestc=lagcors[bestlag],improve=lagcors[bestlag]-c0,slope=slope,std_ratio=np.nanstd(x)/np.nanstd(peer),diff_std_ratio=np.nanstd(dx)/np.nanstd(dp),diff_slope=diff_slope,rmse=np.sqrt(np.nanmean(resid**2)),neighbors=','.join(ids[nn[:5]])))\nm=pd.DataFrame(df).set_index('sid')\nfor col,asc in [('improve',False),('c0',True),('slope',True),('std_ratio',True),('diff_std_ratio',True),('diff_slope',True),('rmse',False)]:\n print('\\\\n',col, 'top')\n print(m.sort_values(col,ascending=asc).head(15)[['poll','district','n','c0','bestlag','bestc','improve','slope','std_ratio','diff_std_ratio','diff_slope','rmse','neighbors']].round(3).to_string())\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

times=sorted(r.timestamp.unique())\nwide=r.pivot(index='station_id',columns='timestamp',values='current_reading').reindex(columns=times)\nfill=wide.T.interpolate(limit=2,limit_area='inside').T\nids=s.index.to_numpy(); pos={x:i for i,x in enumerate(ids)}\nD=np.sqrt(((s.latitude.to_numpy()[:,None]-s.latitude.to_numpy()[None,:])*111)**2+((s.longitude.to_numpy()[:,None]-s.longitude.to_numpy()[None,:])*70)**2)\nwid=wst.index.to_numpy(); DW=np.sqrt(((s.latitude.to_numpy()[:,None]-wst.latitude.to_numpy()[None,:])*111)**2+((s.longitude.to_numpy()[:,None]-wst.longitude.to_numpy()[None,:])*70)**2)\nweather={}\nfor ws in wid:\n weather[ws]=w[w.weather_station_id.eq(ws)].set_index('timestamp').reindex(times)\nrows=[]\nfor sid in wide.index:\n i=pos[sid]\n elig=np.where((poll.reindex(ids).values==poll[sid])&(ids\"'!=sid))[0]\n nn=elig[np.argsort(D[i,elig])]\n for k in [3,5,8,15]:\n  use=nn[:k]; vals=fill.reindex(ids[use]).to_numpy(float); wt=1/np.maximum(D[i,use],.1); valid=np.isfinite(vals)\n  pr=np.nansum(vals*wt[:,None],axis=0)/np.nansum(valid*wt[:,None],axis=0)\n  x=wide.loc[sid].to_numpy(float); ok=np.isfinite(x)&np.isfinite(pr)\n  beta=np.polyfit(pr[ok],x[ok],1); res=x-(beta[0]*pr+beta[1]); raw=x-pr\n  # district peer median\n  de=[z for z in ids if z!=sid and poll.get(z)==poll[sid] and s.loc[z,'\"'district']==s.loc[sid,'district']]\n  dpr=np.nanmedian(fill.reindex(de).to_numpy(float),axis=0); odk=np.isfinite(x)&np.isfinite(dpr); db=np.polyfit(dpr[odk],x[odk],1); dres=x-(db[0]*dpr+db[1])\n  for wchoice in ['nearest','district']:\n   if wchoice=='nearest':\n    ww=weather[wid[np.argmin(DW[i])]]\n   else:\n    wslist=wst.index[wst.district.eq(s.loc[sid,'district'])]\n    ww=w[w.weather_station_id.isin(wslist)].groupby('timestamp')[['temperature_c','humidity_pct','wind_speed_m_s','rain_accumulation_mm']].mean().reindex(times)\n   for rn,rv in [('res',res),('raw',raw),('dres',dres)]:\n    for var in ['temperature_c','humidity_pct','wind_speed_m_s','rain_accumulation_mm']:\n     y=ww[var].to_numpy(float); m=np.isfinite(rv)&np.isfinite(y)\n     if m.sum()>8:\n      cor=np.corrcoef(rv[m],y[m])[0,1]; spear=spearmanr(rv[m],y[m]).statistic\n      rows.append((sid,k,wchoice,rn,var,cor,spear,m.sum()))\nout=pd.DataFrame(rows,columns=['sid','k','weather','resid','var','cor','spear','n'])\n# aggregation strongest across configurations per station: median abs correlation per variable/config and max\nfor resid in ['res','raw','dres']:\n z=out[out.resid.eq(resid)]\n a=z.assign(ac=z.cor.abs()).groupby('sid').ac.max().sort_values(ascending=False)\n print('\\\\nRESID',resid,'max config top')\n for sid in a.head(20).index:\n  q=z[z.sid.eq(sid)].assign(ac=lambda d:d.cor.abs()).sort_values('ac',ascending=False).iloc[0]\n  print(sid,round(q.cor,3),q['var'],q.weather,'k',q.k,'spear',round(q.spear,3),'max',round(a[sid],3))\n# consistent measure mean abs over k/weather for each variable\nz=out[out.resid.eq('res')].assign(ac=lambda d:d.cor.abs())\ng=z.groupby(['sid','var']).agg(med=('ac','median'),min=('ac','min'),max=('ac','max'),cor=('cor','mean')).reset_index()\nprint('\\\\nConsistent by variable top median abs')\nprint(g.sort_values('med',ascending=False).head(40).round(3).to_string(index=False))\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
